The core protocol implementation is in src/auth/cephx/ which contains the main classes and structures.\\n\\nKey Classes and their Files:\\n\\n1. CephxClientHandler (src/auth/cephx/CephxClientHandler.h) - Implements client-side Cephx authentication, extends AuthClientHandler, manages ticket requests and verification\\n\\n2. CephxServiceHandler (src/auth/cephx/CephxServiceHandler.h) - Implements service-side authentication, extends AuthServiceHandler, handles incoming authentication requests and generates session keys\\n\\n3. CephxAuthorizeHandler (src/auth/cephx/CephxAuthorizeHandler.h) - Implements the AuthAuthorizeHandler interface for verifying authorizers on secure connections\\n\\n4. KeyServer (src/auth/cephx/CephxKeyServer.h) - Central key management class handling ticket generation, key rotation, and service secret distribution. Contains key rotation logic for OSD, MDS, and MGR entities\\n\\n5. CephxSessionHandler (src/auth/cephx/CephxSessionHandler.h) - Manages per-session cryptographic operations and message signing\\n\\nProtocol Message Structures (src/auth/cephx/CephxProtocol.h):\\n\\n- CephXAuthorizer - Authorizer structure with nonce, ticket, and session key\\n- CephXTicketHandler - Handles individual service tickets and ticket validity\\n- CephXTicketManager - Manages multiple service tickets and determines when new tickets are needed\\n- CephXServiceTicket - Encrypted ticket containing session key and validity period\\n- CephXServiceTicketInfo - Server-side ticket info with AuthTicket and session key\\n- CephXAuthenticate - Client authentication request with challenges\\n- CephXServerChallenge - Server's challenge value\\n- CephXAuthorizeChallenge - Challenge structure for authorization phase\\n- CephXAuthorizeReply - Server's response to authorization with nonce confirmation\\n\\nBase Authentication Classes (src/auth/):\\n\\n- AuthAuthorizer (Auth.h) - Abstract interface for authorizers across all auth protocols\\n- AuthTicket (Auth.h) - Standard ticket structure with name, global_id, timestamps, and capabilities\\n- RotatingSecrets (Auth.h) - Manages rotating key state for services with 3-key rotation (previous, current, next)\\n- ExpiringCryptoKey (Auth.h) - Key with expiration timestamp\\n- AuthAuthorizeHandler (AuthAuthorizeHandler.h) - Interface for authorization verification on incoming connections\\n- AuthServiceHandler (AuthServiceHandler.h) - Base class for service-side authentication\\n- AuthClientHandler (AuthClientHandler.h) - Base class for client-side authentication\\n- AuthSessionHandler (AuthSessionHandler.h) - Interface for session message signing and verification\\n- RotatingKeyRing (RotatingKeyRing.h) - Manages access to rotating secrets and entity keyrings\\n\\nMonitor-side Files (src/mon/):\\n\\n- AuthMonitor (AuthMonitor.h/cc) - Paxos-based monitor service managing authentication state and key distribution\\n- MonClient (MonClient.h/cc) - Client connection handler for monitors including MonConnection class which manages per-connection authentication handshakes\\n\\nProtocol Message Types (src/messages/):\\n\\n- MAuth (MAuth.h) - Authentication request message with protocol type and payload\\n- MAuthReply (MAuthReply.h) - Authentication reply with protocol, result code, global_id, and response data\\n\\nSupporting Files:\\n\\n- KeyRing (KeyRing.h/cc) - Key Rotation: RotatingSecrets maintains 3-version key rotation with previous, current, and next keys, with automatic expiration and renewal\\n\\n2. Ticket Generation: CephxKeyServer.build_session_auth_info() generates service tickets with session keys encrypted using principal keys\\n\\n3. Mutual Authentication: Challenge-response protocol between client and server using server challenges and client challenges\\n\\n4. Authorization: CephxAuthorizeHandler.verify_authorizer() validates signed authorizer messages on connections\\n\\n5. Capability System: Tickets include AuthCapsInfo encoding service-specific capabilities granted to the entity\\n\\n6.
